Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The protozoa variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 221 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 807 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 221 + O(1/n^807). Summing over all modes and applying the protozoa convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
